Validation happens before the\n * insert, inside the caller's transaction.\n */\nfunction validatePayload(eventType: string, payload: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {\n  if (isEventType(eventType)) {\n    return parseEventPayload(eventType, payload) as Record<string, unknown>;\n  }\n  return objectPayload.parse(payload ?? {});\n}\n\n/**\n * Append-only event ledger (root AGENTS.md invariant #3, scope §9).\n *\n * `append()` is THE single funnel (plan §3.4 / M3 §5): payload validation,\n * category derivation, sequence assignment, event_id idempotency and\n * task/project scope all live here. `recordAgentEvent` & friends are thin\n * wrappers over it. Never called inside its own transaction: the caller\n * (task/checkpoint/decision service, or the ingest service) owns the\n * transaction so the state change and its events commit together.\n */\nexport class EventService {\n  constructor(private db: Database) {}\n\n  /** The canonical funnel. See class doc; all record* methods end here. */\n  append(input: AppendEventInput): AppendedEvent {\n    const payload = validatePayload(input.eventType, input.payload);\n    const category = categoryForEventType(input.eventType);\n\n    if (input.eventId) {\n      const existing = this.findByEventId(input.eventId);\n      if (existing) return { ...toAppended(existing), duplicate: true };\n    }\n\n    const id = uuid();\n    const now = new Date().toISOString();\n    const occurredAt = input.occurredAt ?? now;\n    const sequence = this.nextSequence(input.projectId, input.taskId);\n\n    try {\n      this.db.raw\n        .prepare(\n          `INSERT INTO events (id, project_id, task_id, sequence, actor, event_type, category, source, payload, event_id, occurred_at, created_at)\n           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n        )\n        .run(\n          id,\n          input.projectId,\n          input.taskId ?? null,\n          sequence,\n          input.actor,\n          input.eventType,\n          category,\n          input.source,\n          JSON.stringify(payload),\n          input.eventId ?? null,\n          occurredAt,\n          now,\n        );\n    } catch (err: any) {\n      // Concurrent retry with the same event_id: the unique index won the race.\n      if (input.eventId && String(err?.code ?? '').startsWith('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT')) {\n        const existing = this.findByEventId(input.eventId);\n        if (existing) return { ...toAppended(existing), duplicate: true };\n      }\n      throw err;\n    }\n\n    return {\n      id,\n      event_id: input.eventId ?? null,\n      sequence,\n      created_at: now,\n      occurred_at: occurredAt,\n      duplicate: false,\n    };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Legacy Core path (task/checkpoint/decision services): source `core`,\n   * no client event_id. Kept so existing callers and their tests stay stable.\n   */\n  recordEvent(data: RecordEventInput): RecordedEvent {\n    const result = this.append({\n      projectId: data.projectId,\n      taskId: data.taskId,\n      actor: data.actor,\n      source: 'core',\n      eventType: data.eventType,\n      payload: data.payload,\n    });\n    return { id: result.id, sequence: result.sequence, created_at: result.created_at };\n  }\n\n  /** WS-2 ledger wrappers (plan §3.4) — thin funnels over append(). */\n  recordAgentEvent(\n    input: Omit<AppendEventInput, 'source' | 'taskId'> & { taskId?: string | null },\n  ): AppendedEvent {\n    return this.append({ ...input, taskId: input.taskId ?? null, source: `agent:${input.actor}` });\n  }\n\n  recordProgressEvent(\n    input: Omit<AppendEventInput, 'source' | 'taskId'> & { taskId?: string | null },\n  ): AppendedEvent {\n    return this.append({ ...input, taskId: input.taskId ?? null, source: `agent:${input.actor}` });\n  }\n\n  recordVerificationEvent(\n    input: Omit<AppendEventInput, 'source' | 'taskId'> & { taskId?: string | null },\n  ): AppendedEvent {\n    return this.append({ ...input, taskId: input.taskId ?? null, source: `agent:${input.actor}` });\n  }\n\n  recordRecoveryEvent(\n    input: Omit<AppendEventInput, 'source' | 'taskId'> & { taskId?: string | null },\n  ): AppendedEvent {\n    return this.append({ ...input, taskId: input.taskId ?? null, source: `agent:${input.actor}` });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Deterministic listing (WS-3.1): one scope — `taskId` for a task timeline,\n   * `projectId` for the project-level (task-less) history — plus optional\n   * category/source filters, ordered by the per-scope monotonic sequence.\n   */\n  listEvents(filter: ListEventsFilter) {\n    if (!filter.taskId && !filter.projectId) {\n      throw new ValidationError('listEvents requires exactly one scope: taskId or projectId.');\n    }\n\n    let query = 'SELECT * FROM events WHERE ';\n    const params: any[] = [];\n    if (filter.taskId) {\n      query += 'task_id = ?';\n      params.push(filter.taskId);\n    } else {\n      query += 'project_id = ? AND task_id IS NULL';\n      params.push(filter.projectId);\n    }\n    if (filter.category) {\n      query += ' AND category = ?';\n      params.push(filter.category);\n    }\n    if (filter.source) {\n      query += ' AND source = ?';\n      params.push(filter.source);\n    }\n    query += ' ORDER BY sequence ASC, id ASC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?';\n    params.push(filter.limit ?? 100, filter.offset ?? 0);\n\n    return (this.db.raw.prepare(query).all(...params) as any[]).map(toEventRow);\n  }\n\n  getEventsSince(taskId: string, sinceSequence: number) {\n    return (this.db.raw\n      .prepare('SELECT * FROM events WHERE task_id = ? AND sequence > ? ORDER BY sequence ASC')\n      .all(taskId, sinceSequence) as any[]).map(toEventRow);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Sequence is monotonic per scope: per task, or per project for task-less\n   * events (plan §3.1) — the two namespaces are independent by design.\n   */\n  private nextSequence(projectId: string, taskId: string | null): number {\n    const row = taskId\n      ? (this.db.raw\n          .prepare('SELECT MAX(sequence) AS max_seq FROM events WHERE task_id = ?')\n          .get(taskId) as any)\n      : (this.db.raw\n          .prepare(\n            'SELECT MAX(sequence) AS max_seq FROM events WHERE project_id = ? AND task_id IS NULL',\n          )\n          .get(projectId) as any);\n    return (row?.max_seq || 0) + 1;\n  }\n\n  private findByEventId(eventId: string): Record<string, any> | undefined {\n    return this.db.raw.prepare('SELECT * FROM events WHERE event_id = ?').get(eventId) as any;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction toAppended(row: Record<string, any>): AppendedEvent {\n  return {\n    id: row.id,\n    event_id: row.event_id ?? null,\n    sequence: row.sequence,\n    created_at: row.created_at,\n    occurred_at: row.occurred_at ?? row.created_at,\n    duplicate: true,\n  };\n}\n",
  "path": "backend/packages/core/src/domain/event-service.ts"
}

{
  "content": "import type { Database } from '../db/connection.js';\nimport {\n  isIngestibleType,\n  type IngestEnvelopeInput,\n  type IngestBatchResult,\n  type IngestEventResult,\n} from '@workjournal/shared';\nimport { EventService } from './event-service.js';\nimport { SessionBindingService } from './session-binding-service.js';\n\n/**\n * Ledger-only batch ingestion (plan §10.5 / spec M10, decisions D-023, WS-2.4).\n *\n * - Bounded batch (≤50) enforced by the shared ingestBatchSchema.\n * - Task-lifecycle/transition-shaped payloads are rejected at the route with\n *   400; this service re-checks the allowlist defensively (ledger-only even\n *   if the endpoint is abused).\n * - Idempotency is by client-generated event_id: a resend is a no-op\n *   `duplicate`, never a second row (EventService.append owns the dedup).\n * - Per-envelope result (`accepted` | `duplicate` | `rejected` + reason) so a\n *   partial batch never silently drops events.\n * - task_id backfill: envelopes without task_id are bound to the session's\n *   active task (WS-2.5) when the payload carries a session_id; otherwise\n *   they stay project-level (task_id NULL).\n *\n * The single transition function remains the only path that changes task\n * status — nothing in this service writes to `tasks`.\n */\nexport class IngestService {\n  private events: EventService;\n  private bindings: SessionBindingService;\n  /**\n   * WS-5 seam: progress_fulfilment triggers checkpoint *evaluation* (plan\n   * §3.5/§6). No-op until the WS-5 policy engine lands; recording the event\n   * itself is WS-2's job.\n   */\n  private onFulfilment: ((taskId: string | null, eventId: string) => void) | null = null;\n\n  constructor(\n    private db: Database,\n    private projectId: string,\n  ) {\n    this.events = new EventService(db);\n    this.bindings = new SessionBindingService(db);\n  }\n\n  /** Wire the WS-5 checkpoint-evaluation hook (no-op seam until then). */\n  setFulfilmentHook(hook: (taskId: string | null, eventId: string) => void): void {\n    this.onFulfilment = hook;\n  }\n\n  ingest(envelopes: IngestEnvelopeInput[]): IngestBatchResult {\n    const results: IngestEventResult[] = [];\n\n    // One transaction over the whole batch: accepted events commit together;\n    // per-envelope rejections are validation outcomes, not failures, and do\n    // not abort the batch.\n    this.db.raw.exec('BEGIN');\n    try {\n      for (const envelope of envelopes) {\n        results.push(this.ingestOne(envelope));\n      }\n      this.db.raw.exec('COMMIT');\n    } catch (err) {\n      this.db.raw.exec('ROLLBACK');\n      throw err;\n    }\n\n    return tally(results);\n  }\n\n  private ingestOne(envelope: IngestEnvelopeInput): IngestEventResult {\n    // Defensive ledger-only guard (D-023); the route already 400s these.\n    if (!isIngestibleType(envelope.type)) {\n      return {\n        event_id: envelope.event_id,\n        status: 'rejected',\n        reason: `Event type \"${envelope.type}\" is not ingestible; ingestion accepts ledger signals only.`,\n      };\n    }\n\n    try {\n      const taskId = this.resolveTaskId(envelope);\n      const appended = this.events.append({\n        projectId: this.projectId,\n        taskId,\n        actor: envelope.actor,\n        source: envelope.source,\n        eventType: envelope.type,\n        payload: envelope.payload,\n        eventId: envelope.event_id,\n        occurredAt: envelope.occurred_at ?? null,\n      });\n\n      if (envelope.type === 'progress_fulfilment' && !appended.duplicate) {\n        this.onFulfilment?.(taskId, appended.id);\n      }\n\n      return {\n        event_id: envelope.event_id,\n        status: appended.duplicate ? 'duplicate' : 'accepted',\n        id: appended.id,\n        sequence: appended.sequence,\n        task_id: taskId,\n      };\n    } catch (err: any) {\n      // Payload schema violation (strict per-type schema) or similar —\n      // reported per envelope so the rest of the batch still lands.\n      return {\n        event_id: envelope.event_id,\n        status: 'rejected',\n        reason: err?.message || String(err),\n      };\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Explicit task scope wins; otherwise backfill from the session's active\n   * binding — but only when the binding belongs to THIS project (a session\n   * never silently leaks events into another project's ledger).\n   */\n  private resolveTaskId(envelope: IngestEnvelopeInput): string | null {\n    if (envelope.task_id) return envelope.task_id;\n    const sessionId = extractSessionId(envelope.payload);\n    if (!sessionId) return null;\n    const binding = this.bindings.resolveActive(sessionId);\n    if (binding && binding.projectId === this.projectId) return binding.taskId;\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/** Agent-event payloads carry `session_id` where applicable (M3 §4). */\nfunction extractSessionId(payload: unknown): string | null {\n  if (payload && typeof payload === 'object' && typeof (payload as any).session_id === 'string') {\n    return (payload as any).session_id;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction tally(results: IngestEventResult[]): IngestBatchResult {\n  const accepted = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'accepted').length;\n  const duplicates = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'duplicate').length;\n  const rejected = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'rejected').length;\n  return { results, accepted, duplicates, rejected };\n}\n",
  "path": "backend/packages/core/src/domain/ingest-service.ts"
}

Task-lifecycle or transition-shaped payloads are rejected\n * with 400; per-envelope validation failures come back as `rejected` results\n * so a partial batch never silently drops events. Idempotency is by\n * event_id (D-023), not by an operation-level idempotency key.\n */\nexport async function registerIngestRoutes(server: FastifyInstance) {\n  server.post(\n    '/events',\n    { preHandler: [validateQuery(ingestQuerySchema), validateBody(ingestBatchSchema)] },\n    async (request) => {\n      const { project_id } = request.query as z.infer<typeof ingestQuerySchema>;\n      const body = request.body as IngestBatchInput;\n\n      // Ledger-only contract (D-023): request-level 400 when any envelope\n      // tries to smuggle a task-lifecycle/transition-shaped event in.\n      const offending = [\n        ...new Set(body.events.filter((e) => !isIngestibleType(e.type)).map((e) => e.type)),\n      ];\n      if (offending.length > 0) {\n        throw new ValidationError(\n          `Ledger-only ingestion: these event types are not ingestible: ${offending.join(', ')}. ` +\n            'Task lifecycle and transition events are recorded by Core only.',\n        );\n      }\n\n      // Unknown/unavailable project -> NotFoundError from the resolver.\n      const db = server.projectDbs.forProject(project_id);\n      return new IngestService(db, project_id).ingest(body.events);\n    },\n  );\n}\n",

}

  "content": "export async function callCore<T = any>(\n  baseUrl: string,\n  method: string,\n  path: string,\n  body?: unknown,\n  options?: { headers?: Record<string, string> },\n): Promise<T> {\n  const url = `${baseUrl}${path}`;\n\n  const requestOptions: RequestInit = {\n    method,\n    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...options?.headers },\n  };\n\n  if (body !== undefined && method !== 'GET') {\n    requestOptions.body = JSON.stringify(body);\n  }\n\n  let response: Response;\n  try {\n    response = await fetch(url, requestOptions);\n  } catch (err: any) {\n    // Root AGENTS.md: if the service is down, tell the agent how to start it; no auto-start.\n    throw new Error(\n      `WorkJournal core service is not reachable at ${baseUrl} (${err?.message || err}). ` +\n        `Start it with \"pnpm start\" (service) or \"pnpm dev\" (watch mode) in backend/, then retry.`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  const text = await response.text();\n  let data: any = null;\n  if (text) {\n    try {\n      data = JSON.parse(text);\n    } catch {\n      data = null; // non-JSON body (e.g. text/markdown exports)\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!response.ok) {\n    const code = typeof data?.error === 'string' ? data.error : undefined;\n    const message = data?.message || (typeof data?.error === 'object' && data?.error?.message) ||\n      `Core service error: ${response.status}`;\n    throw new Error(code ? `[${code}] ${message}` : String(message));\n  }\n\n  // Markdown exports come back as text; everything else is JSON.\n  return (data === null ? (text as unknown as T) : (data as T));\n}\n",

}

Add projectIdFromAgentId to actor.ts (agent identity helpers):

```ts
/**
 * Agent IDs are self-declared as `<agent-string>_<project-id>` (root
 * AGENTS.md) and project ids never contain `_`, so the project id is the
 * suffix after the LAST underscore. Returns null when the id carries no
 * project suffix.
 */
export function projectIdFromAgentId(agentId: string): string | null {
  const idx = agentId.lastIndexOf('_');
  if (idx <= 0 || idx === agentId.length - 1) return null;
  return agentId.slice(idx + 1);
}
```

result : JSON.stringify(result) },\n  ],\n});\n\nconst errorMessage = (err: unknown): string =>\n  err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n\n/**\n * Thin forwarding layer (root AGENTS.md): every input schema comes from\n * `@workjournal/shared`, so the tools cannot drift from the HTTP contract, and no\n * business logic lives here.\n */\nexport function registerTools(server: McpServer, coreUrl: string) {\n  const tool = (\n    name: string,\n    description: string,\n    inputSchema: ZodRawShape,\n    handler: (args: any, extra?: any) => Promise<unknown>,\n  ) =>\n    server.registerTool(name, { title: name, description, inputSchema }, async (args: any, extra: any) => {\n      try {\n        return asJson(await handler(args, extra));\n      } catch (err) {\n        return { ...asJson(errorMessage(err)), isError: true };\n      }\n    });\n\n  const query = (params: Record<string, unknown>) => buildQueryString(params);\n\n  /**\n   * Agent-reporting tools (plan §3.5, WS-2.6) post ONE canonical ledger\n   * envelope to the ingestion API (D-023): event_id = idempotency_key,\n   * source = `agent:<agent_id>`, category derived by Core.     .prepare(\n        `INSERT INTO projects (id, name, root_path, created_at, updated_at) VALUES ('p1', 'P1', '/p1', '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z', '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z')`,\n      )\n      .run();\n    db.raw\n      .prepare(\n        `INSERT INTO tasks (id, project_id, title, created_at, updated_at) VALUES ('t1', 'p1', 'T1', '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z', '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z')`,\n      )\n      .run();\n    db.raw\n      .prepare(\n        `INSERT INTO events (id, project_id, task_id, sequence, actor, event_type, payload, created_at)\n         VALUES ('e1', 'p1', 't1', 1, 'op', 'task_created', '{\"title\":\"T1\"}', '2026-01-01T00:00:01.000Z')`,\n      )\n      .run();\n\n    runMigrations(db, PROJECT_MIGRATIONS_DIR);\n\n    const row = db.raw.prepare('SELECT * FROM events WHERE id = ?').get('e1') as any;\n    expect(row.task_id).toBe('t1');\n    expect(row.category).toBe('task');\n    expect(row.source).toBe('core');\n    expect(row.event_id).toBeNull();\n    expect(row.occurred_at).toBe(row.created_at); // backfilled from created_at\n    expect(row.payload).toBe('{\"title\":\"T1\"}'); // data intact through the rebuild\n  });\n\n  it('enforces one {
  "content": "import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';\nimport { rmSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { buildServer, projectDbPath, type Database, type Config } from '@workjournal/core';\nimport { createRegistryDb, tempProjectRoot } from '../helpers/registry-factory.js';\n\n/**\n * WS-2.4 + WS-2.5: POST /api/ingest/events through the real Fastify app\n * (inject) — bounded batch, ledger-only 400, per-envelope\n * accepted/duplicate/rejected, event_id idempotency, session-binding\n * task_id backfill, and project-level (task-less) events.\n */\ndescribe('Ingestion API — POST /api/ingest/events', () => {\n  let registry: Database;\n  let cleanupRegistry: () => void;\n  let server: ReturnType<typeof buildServer>;\n  let projectRoot: string;\n  let projectId: string;\n\n  const config: Config = {\n    workjournalHome: '~/.workjournal-test',\n    host: '127.0.0.1',\n    port: 0,\n    logLevel: 'silent',\n    nodeEnv: 'test',\n  };\n\n  const AGENT = 'mimo_testproj';\n\n  const envelope = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({\n    event_id: `evt-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`,\n    type: 'agent_session_started',\n    source: `agent:${AGENT}`,\n    actor: AGENT,\n    payload: { agent_id: AGENT, session_id: 'sess-1' },\n    ...over,\n  });\n\n  const post = (events: unknown[], query = `project_id=${projectId}`) =>\n    server.inject({\n      method: 'POST',\n      url: `/api/ingest/events?${query}`,\n      payload: { events },\n    });\n\n  const createTask = async (title: string, key: string) => {\n    const res = await server.inject({\n      method: 'POST',\n      url: '/api/tasks',\n      payload: { project_id: projectId, agent_id: AGENT, title, idempotency_key: key },\n    });\n    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);\n    return res.json();\n  };\n\n  beforeEach(async () => {\n    const reg = createRegistryDb();\n    registry = reg.db;\n    cleanupRegistry = reg.cleanup;\n    projectRoot = tempProjectRoot();\n    server = buildServer(registry, config);\n    await server.ready();\n\n    const res = await server.inject({\n      method: 'POST',\n      url: '/api/projects',\n      payload: { name: 'IngestProj', root_path: projectRoot, idempotency_key: 'ingest-proj-001' },\n    });\n    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(201);\n    projectId = res.json().id;\n  });\n\n  afterEach(async () => {\n    await server.close();\n    cleanupRegistry();\n    rmSync(projectRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });\n  });\n\n  it('accepts a valid batch and returns the canonical envelope in listing', async () => {\n    const at = '2026-10-27T05:00:00.000Z';\n    const res = await post([\n      envelope({ occurred_at: at }),\n      envelope({\n        type: 'progress_milestone',\n        payload: { agent_id: AGENT, session_id: 'sess-1', summary: 'M1 done' },\n      }),\n    ]);\n    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);\n    const body = res.json();\n    expect(body.accepted).toBe(2);\n    expect(body.duplicates).toBe(0);\n    expect(body.rejected).toBe(0);\n    expect(body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'accepted', task_id: null, sequence: 1 });\n    expect(body.results[1]).toMatchObject({ status: 'accepted', sequence: 2 });\n\n    const list = await server.inject({ method: 'GET', url: `/api/events?project_id=${projectId}` });\n    expect(list.statusCode).toBe(200);\n    const events = list.json();\n    expect(events).toHaveLength(2);\n    expect(events[0]).toMatchObject({\n      event_type: 'agent_session_started',\n      category: 'agent',\n      source: `agent:${AGENT}`,\n      task_id: null,\n      occurred_at: at,\n    });\n    expect(events[0].event_id).toBe(body.results[0].event_id);\n  });\n\n  it('rejects batches over 50 envelopes', async () => {\n    const batch = Array.from({ length: 51 }, () => envelope());\n    const res = await post(batch);\n    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);\n  });\n\n  it('rejects the whole request with 400 when a task-lifecycle type sneaks in (D-023)', async () => {\n    const res = await post([\n      envelope(),\n      envelope({ type: 'task_transitioned', payload: { from: 'in_progress', to: 'completed' } }),\n    ]);\n    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);\n    expect(res.json().message).toMatch(/ledger-only/i);\n  });\n\n  it('reports per-envelope rejection without dropping the valid ones', async () => {\n    const res = await post([\n      envelope(),\n      envelope({\n        type: 'progress_milestone',\n        payload: { agent_id: AGENT, status: 'completed' }, // strict violation\n      }),\n    ]);\n    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(200);\n    const body = res.json();\n    expect(body.accepted).toBe(1);\n    expect(body.rejected).toBe(1);\n    expect(body.results[1].status).toBe('rejected');\n    expect(body.results[1].reason).toBeTruthy();\n  });\n\n  it('is idempotent by event_id across requests (duplicate, not double)', async () => {\n    const evt = envelope();\n    const first = await post([evt]);\n    expect(first.json().accepted).toBe(1);\n\n    const second = await post([evt]);\n    expect(second.statusCode).toBe(200);\n    expect(second.json().duplicates).toBe(1);\n    expect(second.json().results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'duplicate', sequence: 1 });\n\n    // Same event_id twice inside ONE batch: first accepted, second duplicate.\n    const inBatch = await post([envelope({ event_id: 'twin-key' }), envelope({ event_id: 'twin-key' })]);\n    expect(inBatch.json().accepted).toBe(1);\n    expect(inBatch.json().duplicates).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  it('backfills task_id from the active session binding (WS-2.5)', async () => {\n    const task = await createTask('Bound task', 'ingest-task-001');\n\n    // Claim WITH session_id — as the adapter does when OpenCode sends _meta.\n    const claim = await server.inject({\n      method: 'POST',\n      url: `/api/tasks/${task.id}/claim`,\n      payload: {\n        task_id: task.id,\n        agent_id: AGENT,\n        expected_version: task.version,\n        idempotency_key: 'ingest-claim-001',\n        session_id: 'sess-1',\n      },\n    });\n    expect(claim.statusCode).toBe(200);\n\n    // Plugin event WITHOUT task_id for the same session lands on the task.\n    const res = await post([\n      envelope({\n        type: 'progress_milestone',\n        payload: { agent_id: AGENT, session_id: 'sess-1', summary: 'correlated' },\n      }),\n    ]);\n    expect(res.json().results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'accepted', task_id: task.id });\n\n    const timeline = await server.inject({ method: 'GET', url: `/api/events?task_id=${task.id}` });\n    const types = timeline.json().map((e: any) => e.event_type);\n    expect(types).toContain('progress_milestone');\n\n    // An event for an UNBOUND session stays project-level.\n    const unbound = await post([\n      envelope({\n        payload: { agent_id: AGENT, session_id: 'sess-unknown' },\n      }),\n    ]);\n    expect(unbound.json().results[0].task_id).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it('explicit task_id wins over the binding; releasing the task ends correlation', async () => {\n    const t1 = await createTask('Task one', 'ingest-task-002');\n    const t2 = await createTask('Task two', 'ingest-task-003');\n\n    await server.inject({\n      method: 'POST',\n      url: `/api/tasks/${t1.id}/claim`,\n      payload: { task_id: t1.id, agent_id: AGENT, expected_version: t1.version, idempotency_key: 'ingest-claim-002', session_id: 'sess-2' },\n    });\n\n    // Explicit scope beats the binding.\n    const explicit = await post([\n      envelope({ task_id: t2.id, payload: { agent_id: AGENT, session_id: 'sess-2', summary: 'x' }, type: 'progress_milestone' }),\n    ]);\n    expect(explicit.json().results[0].task_id).toBe(t2.id);\n\n    // Releasing t1 ends its session correlation.\n    await server.inject({\n      method: 'POST',\n      url: `/api/tasks/${t1.id}/release`,\n      payload: { task_id: t1.id, agent_id: AGENT, expected_version: 2, idempotency_key: 'ingest-release-001' },\n    });\n    const after = await post([\n      envelope({ payload: { agent_id: AGENT, session_id: 'sess-2' } }),\n    ]);\n    expect(after.json().results[0].task_id).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it('requires a resolvable project scope', async () => {\n    const noProject = await post([envelope()], 'project_id=does-not-exist');\n    expect(noProject.statusCode).toBe(404);\n\n    const missing = await server.inject({\n      method: 'POST',\n      url: '/api/ingest/events',\n      payload: { events: [envelope()] },\n    });\n    expect(missing.statusCode).toBe(400); // query validation\n  });\n\n  it('rejects source \"core\" and malformed sources', async () => {\n    const asCore = await post([envelope({ source: 'core' })]);\n    expect(asCore.statusCode).toBe(400);\n    const bad = await post([envelope({ source: 'has spaces' })]);\n    expect(bad.statusCode).toBe(400);\n  });\n});\n",
